x0, y1 - y0, z1 - z0)\n\n\n@part\ndef bundle_holder(\n    bundle_diameter=measured(\"bundle_diameter\"),\n    bundle_clearance=0.4,\n    holder_length=12.0,\n    back_thickness=3.0,\n    floor_thickness=2.4,\n    lip_thickness=2.4,\n    lip_extra_height=0.8,\n    screw_wall=2.4,\n    chamfer_size=1.0,\n    draft=False,\n):\n    \"\"\"A wall saddle that carries a cable bundle running horizontally along the wall.\n\n    bundle_diameter: how thick the cable bundle is across\n    bundle_clearance: extra room around the bundle so it drops in instead of being forced\n    holder_length: how much of the bundle the saddle wraps, measured along the run\n    back_thickness: how thick the plate against the wall is\n    floor_thickness: how thick the shelf the bundle rests on is\n    lip_thickness: how thick the front lip that stops the bundle falling out is\n    lip_extra_height: how far the lip reaches past where the bundle can push it\n    screw_wall: how much material rings the screw hole\n    chamfer_size: how big the chamfer on the exposed edges is\n    \"\"\"\n    if bundle_diameter < 2.0:\n        reject(\n            f\"bundle_diameter {bundle_diameter} leaves a channel no printer can open: \"\n            \"raise it above 2.0\",\n            param=\"bundle_diameter\",\n        )\n\n    channel = bundle_diameter + bundle_clearance      # the pocket the bundle drops into\n    radius = channel / 2\n    bundle_radius = bundle_diameter / 2\n\n    # The bundle rides centred in the channel, resting off the wall plate.\n    centre_x = back_thickness + radius\n    centre_z = floor_thickness + radius\n\n    lip_inner = back_thickness + channel\n    front = lip_inner + lip_thickness\n\n    # Pushed 1mm away from the wall the bundle meets the lip over a chord this tall;\n    # the lip carries that whole chord plus a little, so it grips rather than grazes.\n    reach = max(0.0, bundle_radius**2 - max(0.0, radius - 1.0) ** 2) ** 0.5\n    lip_height = centre_z + reach + lip_extra_height\n\n    # The screw sits above the bundle so the head and its driver swing in clear air,\n    # and so a driven screw never crosses the space the bundle occupies.\n    screw_z = centre_z + bundle_radius + SCREW_HEAD_SWING / 2\n    height = screw_z + SCREW_HOLE / 2 + screw_wall\n\n    half = holder_length / 2\n    body = _block(0.0, back_thickness, -half, half, 0.0, height)\n    body += _block(0.0, front, -half, half, 0.0, floor_thickness)\n    body += _block(lip_inner, front, -half, half, 0.0, lip_height)\n\n    bore = Pos(-1.0, 0.0, screw_z) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(\n        SCREW_HOLE / 2, back_thickness + 2.0, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)\n    )\n    body -= bore\n\n    if draft:\n        return body\n\n    # Nothing lying in the wall face or the bed face, and no concave edge: a chamfer\n    # there is a wedge added to the inside corner, not a corner taken off.\n    inside = set(concave_edges(body))\n    box = body.bounding_box()\n    keep = body.edges().filter_by(\n        lambda e: e not in inside\n        and e.bounding_box().max.X > box.min.X + 1e-6\n        and e.bounding_box().max.Z > box.min.Z + 1e-6\n    )\n    return polish(body, keep, chamfer_size)\nPY\nnurb build bundle_holder 2>&1 | tail -20","description":"Write and build the part"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10604,"cache_read_input_tokens":50577,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":10604},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2506692-23ea-4387-bfe2-e1b20f2552f7","uuid":"f0b6de66-2403-4b81-aeaf-bec227d889ad","timestamp":"2026-08-25T21:34:09.657Z","request_id":"req_011CeQBci5B3uXb244Ama7DS"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeQBvGo9eapxpm5W7voKs","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JTXzhvihLoNUMarJWhqute","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /var/folders/x6/7ftjsrq95yg91h14lv_x5hpm0000gn/T/nurb-eval-trial-q3xb1nwm/project && cat > parts/bundle_holder.md <<'MD'\n# bundle_holder\n\n## What it is\n\nA wall saddle for a cable bundle that runs horizontally along a wall. The flat back\nplate goes against the wall and takes one M4 pan-head screw; a shelf runs forward from\nthe bottom of that plate and a lip stands up at its front, so the bundle rests in an\nopen-topped channel that is closed downward and forward. The bundle drops in from above\nand threads along its run; nothing has to be unclipped to add or remove a cable.\n\nOff the bed as modelled: back plate at minimum X, bundle running along Y, down is -Z.\nPrints flat in that same orientation, support-free, no floating features.\n\n## Design notes\n\n- **Channel is `bundle_diameter + bundle_clearance`**, 8.4 across for the measured 8.0\n  bundle. Everything else is derived from those two numbers, so the part rebuilds\n  correctly from roughly 4mm to well past 12mm of bundle without any other edit.\n- **Lip height is computed, not chosen.** Pushed 1mm away from the wall the bundle meets\n  the lip over a chord `sqrt(bundle_r^2 - (channel_r - 1)^2)` tall, 2.4mm at 8mm bundle.\n  The lip carries that whole chord plus `lip_extra_height`, so it grips across the\n  contact band instead of grazing its bottom edge. It stops short of the bundle's top so\n  the bundle still drops in.\n- **Floor and lip are 2.4mm**, the doctrine's minimum wall rather than anything thinner,\n  because those two are the only things holding the bundle up and in.\n- **The screw sits above the bundle, one head-radius clear of it.** `screw_z` is the\n  bundle's top plus half the 8.4 head-and-driver swing, so a driven M4 and the bundle\n  never share space and the driver has clear air all the way out in +X. Nothing of the\n  part stands in front of the plate at that height: the lip tops out well below it.\n- **Back plate is 3.0mm**, above the 2.4 the screw needs behind the seat, and it runs\n  tall enough that the whole head footprint lands on flat seat face rather than running\n  out into the top chamfer (that is the `max()` on `height`).\n- **Projection 10.8mm against a 20.2mm back**, a ratio of 0.53, so the load path rules\n  say chamfers only: no gussets, no ribs.\n- **Chamfer is 1.2mm rather than the usual 1.0.** At 1.0 the corner triangles where three\n  chamfers meet at the top front corners come out at 0.87mm2 and read as slivers. 1.2\n  puts the same triangles at 1.25mm2, over the 1mm2 floor, so the part carries no earned\n  slivers at all and the `[accepted]` block stays empty.\n- Volume is 1135mm3 at the measured 8mm bundle.\n\n## Don't\n\n- **Don't chamfer the bore mouth.** It is a mating surface for the screw, and the 45\n  degree cone leaves a wedge against the bore wall that `min_wall` correctly reports as a\n  0.66mm section. Circle-geometry edges are excluded from the polish set for this reason;\n  do not \"fix\" that filter.\n- **Don't chamfer the concave edges** where the shelf meets the plate and the lip, and\n  don't add the doctrine's 3mm inside-corner relief there either: that corner is the\n  channel, and relief in it eats the bundle's clearance. The junction is in compression\n  under a hanging bundle, so it does not need the relief.\n- **Don't roof the channel into a closed tunnel.** It was considered. The roof bridges\n  8.4mm, it forces the screw a further 4mm up the plate to keep the driver's swing clear,\n  and the two together push the part from 1135 to about 1570mm3 for retention the wall,\n  the shelf and the lip already provide.\n- **Don't lean the lip inward to capture the bundle.** An inward lean is an overhang over\n  an open channel and would need support inside the one pocket nobody can reach.\n- **Don't shrink `bundle_clearance` below 0.4.** The bundle is taped, not machined, and\n  the channel is the one dimension that has to be generous.\n- **Don't add a second screw.** The load is straight shear a few millimetres off the wall,\n  which one M4 carries; a second hole is 8.4mm of head swing that would have to be kept\n  clear of the bundle all over again.\n\n## Changelog\n\n- 2026-08-25: First version. 8.0mm bundle from `measurements.toml`, one M4 pan head,\n  1135mm3, `nurb check` clean.\n\n```toml\n[part]\nmin_wall = 2.4\nforward = [1, 0, 0]\n\n[accepted]\nsliver = 0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_6mm]\nnote = \"The same saddle for a thinner bundle, about 6mm across.\"\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_6mm.params]\nbundle_diameter = 6.0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_12mm]\nnote = \"The same saddle for a fat bundle, about 12mm across.\"\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_12mm.params]\nbundle_diameter = 12.0\n```\nMD\nnurb check bundle_holder 2>&1 | tail -12","description":"Write card with variants and check"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":288,"cache_read_input_tokens":76288,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":288},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2506692-23ea-4387-bfe2-e1b20f2552f7","uuid":"a78befb2-4cd4-461b-9054-904add013c57","timestamp":"2026-08-25T21:38:15.338Z","request_id":"req_011CeQBvFxYaLjMM8nG5e5cq"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01187AH1NEGYah3Gvx1WBoJe","type":"tool_result","content":"from nurb import *\n\nSCREW_HOLE = 4.4          # M4 clearance, medium column\nSCREW_HEAD_SWING = 8.4    # pan head plus driver socket, must stay clear ahead of the seat\n\n\ndef _block(x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1):\n    \"\"\"An axis-aligned block given by its two corners.\"\"\"\n    return Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * Box(x1 - x0, y1 - y0, z1 - z0)\n\n\n@part\ndef bundle_holder(\n    bundle_diameter=measured(\"bundle_diameter\"),\n    bundle_clearance=0.4,\n    holder_length=12.0,\n    back_thickness=3.0,\n    floor_thickness=2.4,\n    lip_thickness=2.4,\n    lip_extra_height=0.8,\n    screw_wall=2.4,\n    chamfer_size=1.2,\n    draft=False,\n):\n    \"\"\"A wall saddle that carries a cable bundle running horizontally along the wall.\n\n    bundle_diameter: how thick the cable bundle is across\n    bundle_clearance: extra room around the bundle so it drops in instead of being forced\n    holder_length: how much of the bundle the saddle wraps, measured along the run\n    back_thickness: how thick the plate against the wall is\n    floor_thickness: how thick the shelf the bundle rests on is\n    lip_thickness: how thick the front lip that stops the bundle falling out is\n    lip_extra_height: how far the lip reaches past where the bundle can push it\n    screw_wall: how much material rings the screw hole\n    chamfer_size: how big the chamfer on the exposed edges is\n    \"\"\"\n    if bundle_diameter < 2.0:\n        reject(\n            f\"bundle_diameter {bundle_diameter} leaves a channel no printer can open: \"\n            \"raise it above 2.0\",\n            param=\"bundle_diameter\",\n        )\n\n    channel = bundle_diameter + bundle_clearance      # the pocket the bundle drops into\n    radius = channel / 2\n    bundle_radius = bundle_diameter / 2\n\n    # The bundle rides centred in the channel, resting off the wall plate.\n    centre_x = back_thickness + radius\n    centre_z = floor_thickness + radius\n\n    lip_inner = back_thickness + channel\n    front = lip_inner + lip_thickness\n\n    # Pushed 1mm away from the wall the bundle meets the lip over a chord this tall;\n    # the lip carries that whole chord plus a little, so it grips rather than grazes.\n    reach = max(0.0, bundle_radius**2 - max(0.0, radius - 1.0) ** 2) ** 0.5\n    lip_height = centre_z + reach + lip_extra_height\n\n    # The screw sits above the bundle so the head and its driver swing in clear air,\n    # and so a driven screw never crosses the space the bundle occupies.\n    screw_z = centre_z + bundle_radius + SCREW_HEAD_SWING / 2\n    # Tall enough to ring the bore, and tall enough that the seat face carries the whole\n    # head footprint rather than running out into the top chamfer.\n    height = max(\n        screw_z + SCREW_HOLE / 2 + screw_wall,\n        screw_z + SCREW_HEAD_SWING / 2 + chamfer_size,\n    )\n\n    half = holder_length / 2\n    body = _block(0.0, back_thickness, -half, half, 0.0, height)\n    body += _block(0.0, front, -half, half, 0.0, floor_thickness)\n    body += _block(lip_inner, front, -half, half, 0.0, lip_height)\n\n    bore = Pos(-1.0, 0.0, screw_z) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(\n        SCREW_HOLE / 2, back_thickness + 2.0, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)\n    )\n    body -= bore\n\n    if draft:\n        return body\n\n    # Nothing lying in the wall face or the bed face, and no concave edge: a chamfer\n    # there is a wedge added to the inside corner, not a corner taken off.\n    inside = set(concave_edges(body))\n    box = body.bounding_box()\n    keep = body.edges().filter_by(\n        lambda e: e not in inside\n        and e.geom_type != GeomType.CIRCLE\n        and e.bounding_box().max.X > box.min.X + 1e-6\n        and e.bounding_box().max.Z > box.min.Z + 1e-6\n    )\n    return polish(body, keep, chamfer_size)\n=== \n  printer: bambu_p1s (printer.toml)\n  bundle_holder: clean\n  bundle_holder_6mm: clean\n  bundle_holder_12mm: clean\n  bundle_holder  13.80 x 12.00 x 20.20 mm  1134.6mm3  1 solid, 27 faces, 59 edges\n  building +Z, bed plane at 0.00, 1 face(s) on it\n\n  findings  none\n\n  faces  showing 12 of 27, largest first. droop is degrees\n      from the build direction, as the overhang rule measures it: 0 is a wall, +90 a ceiling,\n      negative faces up. the pocket the bundle drops into\n    radius = channel / 2\n    bundle_radius = bundle_diameter / 2\n\n    # The bundle rides centred in the channel, resting off the wall plate.\n    centre_x = back_thickness + radius\n    centre_z = floor_thickness + radius\n\n    lip_inner = back_thickness + channel\n    front = lip_inner + lip_thickness\n\n    # Pushed 1mm away from the wall the bundle meets the lip over a chord this tall;\n    # the lip carries that whole chord plus a little, so it grips rather than grazes.\n    reach = max(0.0, bundle_radius**2 - max(0.0, radius - 1.0) ** 2) ** 0.5\n    lip_height = centre_z + reach + lip_extra_height\n\n    # The screw sits above the bundle so the head and its driver swing in clear air,\n    # and so a driven screw never crosses the space the bundle occupies.\n    screw_z = centre_z + bundle_radius + SCREW_HEAD_SWING / 2\n    # Tall enough to ring the bore, and tall enough that the seat face carries the whole\n    # head footprint rather than running out into the top chamfer.\n    height = max(\n        screw_z + SCREW_HOLE / 2 + screw_wall,\n        screw_z + SCREW_HEAD_SWING / 2 + chamfer_size,\n    )\n\n    half = holder_length / 2\n    body = _block(0.0, back_thickness, -half, half, 0.0, height)\n    body += _block(0.0, front, -half, half, 0.0, floor_thickness)\n    body += _block(lip_inner, front, -half, half, 0.0, lip_height)\n\n    bore = Pos(-1.0, 0.0, screw_z) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(\n        SCREW_HOLE / 2, back_thickness + 2.0, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)\n    )\n    body -= bore\n\n    if draft:\n        return body\n\n    #
